}function applyMove(itemId,type){if(isMoveSaving)return;const btn=document.getElementById("moveSaveBtn");if(btn){btn.disabled=true

;const before=latestTotal(itemId),qty=yen(pack*cases);let after=before;if(type==="入庫")after=yen(before+qty);if(type==="出庫"){

function printNormalPackList(itemId,dayKeys){const packs=[]
;(db.records||[]).filter(r=>r.itemId===itemId&&r.createdAt&&dayKeys.includes(ymdKey(new Date(r.createdAt)))).forEach(r=>(r.lines||[]).filter(l=>!l.detail&&Number(l.pack)>0).forEach(l=>{

;const match=normalized.match(/-?(?:\d+(?:\.\d+)?|\.\d+)/);if(!match)return null;const n=Number(match[0]);return Number.isFinite(n)&&n>0?n:null}

;const unit=row?.unit||qrUnitForContainer(row?.containerType),count=`${fmt(qty)}${unit}`,packKg=Number(row?.packWeightKg)
;return packKg>0?`${fmt(qty*packKg)}kg ${count}`:`${count}（kg未設定）`}async function qrStockRowsForRoom(room){const locations=qrCountLocations(room)
;if(!locations.length)return[];const pageSize=1000,lotsByKey=new Map;let offset=0;for(;;){

;if(!Array.isArray(page))throw new Error("QRロット一覧を取得できませんでした");for(const lot of page){const key=String(lot?.qr_key||"").trim()
;if(key)lotsByKey.set(key,lot)}if(page.length<pageSize)break;offset+=pageSize}const grouped=new Map;for(const lot of lotsByKey.values()){

;if(!(qty>0)||!locations.includes(location))continue
;const name=String(lot?.item_name||"（品名未設定）").trim()||"（品名未設定）",containerType=String(lot?.container_type||"").trim()||"（荷姿未設定）",packWeight=String(lot?.pack_weight??"").trim(),unit=qrLotUnit(lot),key=[name,containerType,location,packWeight,unit].join(""),kgOnly=!qrIsLargeContainer(containerType)&&qrStockIsKgUnit(unit)
;let row=grouped.get(key);if(!row){row={name,containerType,location,packWeight,packWeightKg:qrStockPackWeightNumber(packWeight),quantity:0,kgOnly,unit
};grouped.set(key,row)}row.quantity+=qty;row.kgOnly=row.kgOnly&&kgOnly}

}async function printQrInventoryRows(group){const room=group==="room3"?"room3":"room2";return qrStockRowsForRoom(room)}function qrLotCurrentKg(lot){

;if(Number.isFinite(current)&&current>0)return current;const large=qrIsLargeContainer(lot?.container_type);if(large){

function qrResolveInventoryItem(lot){const items=activeItems(),n=qrNormalizeName(lot&&lot.item_name);if(qrIsPotatoVarietyName(n))return{item:null,
rule:QR_POTATO_VARIETY_RULE,reason:"じゃがいも品種名を優先"};const id=String(lot&&lot.item_id||"");const byId=id?items.filter(x=>String(x.id)===id):[]
;if(byId.length===1)return{item:byId[0],method:"item_id"};if(!n)return{item:null,reason:"品目名なし"}
;const exact=items.filter(x=>qrNormalizeName(x.name)===n);if(exact.length===1)return{item:exact[0],method:"品目名完全一致"};if(exact.length>1)return{
item:null,reason:"同名通常品目が複数"};const rule=qrManagedRuleForName(n);if(!rule)return{item:null,reason:"QR対象ルール未一致"}
;const base=qrNormalizeName(rule.name||rule.item_name);const canonical=items.filter(x=>qrNormalizeName(x.name)===base);if(canonical.length===1)return{
item:canonical[0],method:"QR対象別名"};return{item:null,rule,reason:canonical.length?"基準通常品目が複数":"基準通常品目なし"}}
async function qrInventoryKgByItemForRoom(room){const locations=qrCountLocations(room),pageSize=1000,byKey=new Map;let offset=0;for(;;){

;if(!Array.isArray(page))throw new Error("QRロット一覧を取得できませんでした");page.forEach(l=>byKey.set(String(l.qr_key||l.id),l));if(page.length<pageSize)break

;const kg=qrLotCurrentKg(lot);if(!(kg>0)){unconvertible.push({...lot,_qrResolveReason:"kg換算不可"});continue}const resolved=qrResolveInventoryItem(lot)
;if(resolved.item){totals[resolved.item.id]=(totals[resolved.item.id]||0)+kg;continue}const rule=resolved.rule||qrManagedRuleForName(lot.item_name)
;if(rule){const identity=qrStandaloneInventoryIdentity(lot,rule),key=identity.key;if(!standaloneTotals[key])standaloneTotals[key]={name:identity.name,

function printInventoryRows(group,dayKeys=[]){const rows=[];const addNormal=it=>{if(isDetailItem(it)){
const details=(db.itemDetails&&db.itemDetails[it.id]||[]).filter(x=>x.active!==false);if(details.length){details.forEach((l,i)=>rows.push({

sort:Number(it.sort||0)+i/1000,group:"適正在庫品目"}));return}}const packs=printNormalPackList(it.id,dayKeys)
;if(!packs.length&&Number(it.pack)>0)packs.push(Number(it.pack));rows.push({kind:"normal",id:it.id,name:it.name,packs,

;allSortedItems().filter(it=>it.active!==false).forEach(it=>{const room=coldRoomForNormalItem(it);if(group==="room3"){if(room==="room3")addNormal(it)
}else{if(room==="room2")addNormal(it)}});if(group==="room2plus"){
specialItems("exchange").filter(it=>coldRoomForSpecialItem("exchange",it)==="room2").forEach((it,i)=>rows.push({kind:"special",tableId:"exchange",
id:it.id,name:it.name,spec:it.spec||it.unit||"",origin:it.origin||"",sort:10000+(it.sort||i),group:"その他"}))
;specialItems("kataoka").filter(it=>coldRoomForSpecialItem("kataoka",it)==="room2").forEach((it,i)=>rows.push({kind:"special",tableId:"kataoka",
id:it.id,name:it.name,spec:it.spec||it.unit||"",origin:it.origin||"",sort:11000+(it.sort||i),group:"片岡在庫"}))}else if(group==="room3"){
for(const tableId of Object.keys(SPECIAL_STOCK_TABLES)){

name:it.name,spec:it.spec||it.unit||"",origin:it.origin||"",sort:10000+(tableId==="kataoka"?1000:0)+(it.sort||i),
group:tableId==="kataoka"?"片岡在庫":"その他"}))}}
return rows.sort((a,b)=>String(a.group).localeCompare(String(b.group),"ja")||a.sort-b.sort||String(a.name).localeCompare(String(b.name),"ja"))}

function weeklyTableHtml(k,recs,spRecs,open=false,room=weeklyRoom,qrTotals={},qrStandaloneTotals={}){
const s=new Date(k+"T00:00:00"),days=[...Array(7)].map((_,i)=>{const d=new Date(s);d.setDate(s.getDate()+i);return d}),dayKeys=days.map(d=>ymdKey(d))
;const normalMap={},specialMap={};recs.slice().sort((a,b)=>new Date(a.createdAt||0)-new Date(b.createdAt||0)).forEach(r=>{

;if(isDetail)normalMap[key]=mergedDailyInventoryRecord(normalMap[key],r);else if(!normalMap[key]||new Date(r.createdAt)>new Date(normalMap[key].createdAt))normalMap[key]=r
});spRecs.forEach(r=>{const dk=ymdKey(new Date(r.createdAt));if(!dayKeys.includes(dk))return;const key=r.tableId+"_"+r.itemId+"_"+dk
;if(!specialMap[key]||new Date(r.createdAt)>new Date(specialMap[key].createdAt))specialMap[key]=r})
;const displayRows=weeklyTableRowsForDisplay(weeklyDisplayRows(room,qrStandaloneTotals),dayKeys,normalMap,specialMap,qrTotals,qrStandaloneTotals),today=todayKey()
;const body=displayRows.map(row=>{const cells=dayKeys.map(dk=>{if(row.kind==="qr_only"){

}if(row.kind==="normal_detail"){

}if(row.kind==="normal_detail_total"||row.kind==="normal"){

;if(!r&&!qr)return`<td class="${dk===today?"today ":""}unset">-</td>`
;return`<td class="${dk===today?"today ":""}${r?st:"normal"}"><span class="stockVal">${fmt(shown)}kg</span>${qr?`<br><span class="small">通常 ${fmt(base)} + QR ${fmt(qr)}kg</span>`:""}${r?`<br><span class="badge ${st}">${label(st)}</span>`:""}</td>`
}const r=specialMap[row.tableId+"_"+row.id+"_"+dk]
;return`<td class="${dk===today?"today ":""}${r?"normal":"unset"}">${r?`<span class="stockVal">${fmt(r.qty)}${esc(r.unit||row.item.unit||"")}</span>`:"-"}</td>`
}).join("");return`<tr class="${row.group==='kataoka'?'stockRowKataoka':''}"><td>${row.group==='kataoka'?'<span class="stockKataokaBadge">片岡在庫</span> ':''}${esc(row.name)}</td>${cells}</tr>`}).join("")
;return`<div class="card"><div class="itemHead"><div><h2 style="margin:0">${ymd(s)}〜${ymd(days[6])}</h2><div class="small">${roomLabel(room)} / 表示 ${displayRows.length}行 / 当日のQR kgは通常品目の合計として小さく内訳表示します。</div></div><button class="btn btn2" onclick="toggleWeek('${k}')">開閉</button></div><div id="week_${k}" class="weeklyWrap" style="display:${open?"block":"none"}"><table class="weeklyTable"><thead><tr><th>品目名</th>${days.map(d=>`<th class="${ymdKey(d)===today?"today":""}">${d.toLocaleDateString("ja-JP",{

const n=new Date(at||0).getTime();return Number.isFinite(n)?n:0}function adminHistoryText(v,max=160){

/* ===== 本物の .xlsx 出力（在庫表履歴・廃棄一覧 / 週ごとシート分け） ===== */function ensureXlsxReady(){if(typeof XLSX==="undefined"){
alert("Excel出力ライブラリの読み込みに失敗しました。通信環境を確認して再読み込みしてください。");return false}return true}function monthInputValue(d){
return`${d.getFullYear()}-${String(d.getMonth()+1).padStart(2,"0")}`}function monthRangeFromMonthValue(v){
const[y,m]=String(v||monthInputValue(new Date)).split("-").map(Number);const s=new Date(y,m-1,1),e=new Date(y,m,0);return{start:ymdKey(s),
end:ymdKey(e),year:y,month:m}}function dateFromKey(k){return new Date(String(k)+"T00:00:00")}function sheetDateLabel(k){const d=dateFromKey(k)
;return`${String(d.getMonth()+1).padStart(2,"0")}月${String(d.getDate()).padStart(2,"0")}日`}function safeSheetName(n){
return String(n).replace(/[\\/?*\[\]:]/g,"").slice(0,31)}function splitRangeByWeekKeys(startKey,endKey){const weeks=[]
;let cur=dateFromKey(startKey),end=dateFromKey(endKey),no=1;while(cur<=end){
const ws=new Date(cur),we=new Date(cur),dow=we.getDay(),add=dow===0?0:7-dow;we.setDate(we.getDate()+add);if(we>end)we.setTime(end.getTime())
;const s=ymdKey(ws),e=ymdKey(we);weeks.push({weekNo:no,start:s,end:e,sheetName:safeSheetName(`第${no}週_${sheetDateLabel(s)}-${sheetDateLabel(e)}`)})
;cur=new Date(we);cur.setDate(cur.getDate()+1);no++}return weeks}function aoaSheet(aoa,widths){const ws=XLSX.utils.aoa_to_sheet(aoa);if(ws["!ref"]){
const range=XLSX.utils.decode_range(ws["!ref"]);ws["!autofilter"]={ref:XLSX.utils.encode_range(range)};ws["!freeze"]={xSplit:0,ySplit:1}
;for(let R=range.s.r;R<=range.e.r;R++){for(let C=range.s.c;C<=range.e.c;C++){const a=XLSX.utils.encode_cell({r:R,c:C}),cell=ws[a];if(!cell)continue
;cell.s={font:{name:"Yu Gothic",sz:11,bold:R===0,color:{rgb:R===0?"102A43":"000000"}},alignment:{vertical:"center",horizontal:R===0?"center":"left",
wrapText:true},border:{top:{style:"thin",color:{rgb:"BFBFBF"}},bottom:{style:"thin",color:{rgb:"BFBFBF"}},left:{style:"thin",color:{rgb:"BFBFBF"}},
right:{style:"thin",color:{rgb:"BFBFBF"}}},fill:R===0?{patternType:"solid",fgColor:{rgb:"D9EAF7"}}:undefined}}}ws["!cols"]=(widths||[]).map(w=>({wch:w
}))}return ws}function setCellFill(ws,row,col,rgb){const a=XLSX.utils.encode_cell({r:row,c:col});if(!ws[a])return;ws[a].s=ws[a].s||{};ws[a].s.fill={
patternType:"solid",fgColor:{rgb}};ws[a].s.alignment={...ws[a].s.alignment||{},horizontal:"center",vertical:"center"}}
function downloadWorkbookXlsx(wb,filename){XLSX.writeFile(wb,filename,{bookType:"xlsx",cellStyles:true})}function inventoryProperText(r){
const s=r.setting||db.settings?.[r.itemId]||{};return s&&s.min!==undefined&&s.max!==undefined?`${fmt(s.min)}〜${fmt(s.max)}`:""}
function inventoryDiffValue(r){const s=r.setting||db.settings?.[r.itemId]||{},t=Number(r.total||0)
;if(s&&s.min!==undefined&&t<Number(s.min))return yen(t-Number(s.min));if(s&&s.max!==undefined&&t>Number(s.max))return yen(t-Number(s.max));return 0}
function inventoryLineText(r){
return(r.lines||[]).map(l=>l.detail?`${l.spec||""}${l.origin?"/"+l.origin:""} ${fmt(Number(l.subtotal||detailSubtotal(l)))}kg`:`${fmt(l.pack)}kg×${fmt(l.cases)}ケース`).filter(Boolean).join(" / ")
}function downloadInventoryHistoryXlsx(monthKey,rows,filename){if(!ensureXlsxReady())return
;const range=monthRangeFromMonthValue(String(monthKey).slice(0,7));const wb=XLSX.utils.book_new()
;const sorted=rows.slice().sort((a,b)=>new Date(a.createdAt||0)-new Date(b.createdAt||0));splitRangeByWeekKeys(range.start,range.end).forEach(w=>{

;const ws=aoaSheet(aoa,[12,18,12,14,12,10,32,14,20,14]);for(let i=1;i<aoa.length;i++){if(aoa[i][5]==="不足")setCellFill(ws,i,5,"F4CCCC")
;if(aoa[i][5]==="過多")setCellFill(ws,i,5,"FFF2CC");if(aoa[i][5]==="適正")setCellFill(ws,i,5,"E2F0D9")}XLSX.utils.book_append_sheet(wb,ws,w.sheetName)})
;downloadWorkbookXlsx(wb,filename)}function downloadWasteXlsx(startDate,endDate,rows,filename){if(!ensureXlsxReady())return
;const wb=XLSX.utils.book_new(),sorted=rows.slice().sort((a,b)=>String(a.date||"").localeCompare(String(b.date||""))||new Date(a.createdAt||0)-new Date(b.createdAt||0)),headers=["日付","品名","数量kg","単価（円/kg）","金額（円）","価格採用区分","価格採用根拠","産地","仕入先","備考","入力者","入力日時"],toRow=r=>{const qty=Number(r.qty),unit=Number(r.unitPrice),safeQty=Number.isFinite(qty)?qty:0,safeUnit=Number.isFinite(unit)&&unit>0?unit:0;return[r.date||"",r.itemName||"",safeQty,safeUnit||"",safeUnit?yen(safeQty*safeUnit):"",r.priceMatchType||(safeUnit?"手動入力":"未計算"),r.priceMatchReason||"",r.origin||"",r.supplier||"",r.memo||"",r.user||"",r.createdAt?dt(r.createdAt):""]},detailRows=sorted.map(toRow),detailStart=7,detailEnd=detailStart+detailRows.length-1,totalQty=detailRows.reduce((sum,row)=>sum+Number(row[2]||0),0),totalAmount=detailRows.reduce((sum,row)=>sum+Number(row[4]||0),0),whole=[["廃棄一覧（期間全体）","","","","","","","","","","",""],["対象期間",wastePeriodLabel(startDate,endDate),"","","","","","","","","",""],["全品目 合計kg",totalQty,"","全品目 合計金額（円）",totalAmount,"","","","","","",""],["件数",sorted.length,"","","","","","","","","",""],["","","","","","","","","","","",""],headers,...detailRows],widths=[12,18,12,15,15,18,45,12,18,28,14,20],wholeWs=aoaSheet(whole,widths)
;wholeWs["!autofilter"]={ref:`A${detailStart}:L${Math.max(detailStart,detailEnd)}`};wholeWs["!freeze"]={xSplit:0,ySplit:detailStart};XLSX.utils.book_append_sheet(wb,wholeWs,"期間全体")
;splitRangeByWeekKeys(startDate,endDate).forEach(w=>{const wr=sorted.filter(r=>String(r.date||"")>=w.start&&String(r.date||"")<=w.end),ws=aoaSheet([headers,...wr.map(toRow)],widths);XLSX.utils.book_append_sheet(wb,ws,w.sheetName)});wb.Workbook=wb.Workbook||{};wb.Workbook.CalcPr={fullCalcOnLoad:true,forceFullCalc:true,calcMode:"auto"};downloadWorkbookXlsx(wb,filename)}function monthStartKey(d){

function v199QrSnapshotAtDayEnd(lot,movements,dayKey){const received=v163LotReceivedKey(lot),today=todayKey()
;if(!received||dayKey<received||dayKey>today)return null
;const end=new Date(dayKey+"T23:59:59.999+09:00").getTime(),ordered=(movements||[]).filter(m=>{const at=v163MovementTime(m);return at>0&&at<=end
}).sort((a,b)=>v163MovementTime(a)-v163MovementTime(b))
;let qty=Number(lot.received_qty??lot.initial_quantity??0),location=String(lot.storage_location||"").trim(),deleted=false,initialInboundSkipped=false
;for(const m of ordered){const movementLocation=String(m.storage_location||"").trim();if(movementLocation)location=movementLocation
;if(v163IsDeleteMovement(m)){qty=0;deleted=true;continue}const after=v163FiniteNumber(m.after_quantity);if(after!==null){qty=after;deleted=false
;continue}const amount=Math.abs(Number(m.qty??m.quantity??0)),type=String(m.movement_type||"");if(!(amount>=0))continue;if(type.includes("取消")){
const before=v163FiniteNumber(m.before_quantity);if(before!==null)qty=before
}else if(type.includes("廃棄")||type.includes("出庫")||type.includes("調整減"))qty-=amount;else if(type.includes("入庫")||type.includes("調整増")){
const before=v163FiniteNumber(m.before_quantity),sameInitial=!initialInboundSkipped&&type.includes("入庫")&&(before===null||before===0)&&Math.abs(amount-Number(lot.received_qty??lot.initial_quantity??0))<1e-9&&v199QrMovementDateKey(m)===received
;if(sameInitial)initialInboundSkipped=true;else qty+=amount}}return{qty:Math.max(0,yen(qty)),location,deleted}}function v199QrStatusAt(qty,deleted){
